\displaystyle-v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{\epsilon}-{L}_{\pm}f^{\epsilon},

\displaystyle g^{\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\Gamma}_{\pm}\left(f^{\epsilon},f^{\epsilon}\right)\pm\frac{1}{2}E^{\epsilon}\cdot vf^{\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp E^{\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp\epsilon\left(v\times\mathfrak{B}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp\epsilon\left(v\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}_{\pm},

\displaystyle\mathbb{A}_{mj}(g)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(v_{m}v_{j}-1\right)\mu^{1/2}gdv,

(3.3)

\displaystyle\mathbb{B}_{j}(g)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{10}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left(|v|^{2}-5\right)v_{j}\mu^{1/2}gdv,

\displaystyle G^{\epsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle v\mu^{1/2},\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\cdot q_{1}\right\rangle.

The following lemma is concerned with the dissipative effect of the macroscopic quantities such as
{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}(t,x,v)
and
a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}(t,x)-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}(t,x)
, which can be proved by repeating the argument used in the proof of
[
23
, Lemma 3.2]
, we thus omit the details for brevity.

Lemma 3.2
.

For
N\geq 2
,
there exists
G_{f^{\epsilon}}(t)
satisfying

G_{f^{\epsilon}}(t)\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

such that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}G_{f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}(t)-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}(t)\right\|^{2}

(3.4)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Our next result focuses on the dissipative effect of the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{\epsilon}(t,x),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
.

Lemma 3.3
.

For
N\geq 2
,
there exists
G_{E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)
satisfying

G_{E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}

such that the following estimate

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}G_{E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)+\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}(t)+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}(t)\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[E^{\epsilon}(t),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}

(3.5)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle 2E^{\epsilon}+2\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\partial_{t}G^{\epsilon}+\nabla_{x}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathbb{A}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\cdot q_{1}\right)-E^{\epsilon}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)

\displaystyle-2\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}-\left\langle[v,-v]\mu^{1/2},Lf^{\epsilon}+\Gamma\left(f^{\epsilon},f^{\epsilon}\right)\right\rangle,

multiplying the above identity by
E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}
and by integrating the result with respect to
x
over
\mathbb{R}_{x}^{3}
, we can get from Lemma
5.2
that

\displaystyle 2\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}=2\epsilon^{2}\left(\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\},\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left(\partial_{t}G^{\epsilon}(t),\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)+\epsilon^{2}\left(\left\{\nabla_{x}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)+\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathbb{A}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\cdot q_{1}\right)\right\},\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\left(E^{\epsilon}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)+2\epsilon b\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}+\left\langle[v,-v]\mu^{1/2},Lf^{\epsilon}+\Gamma\left(f^{\epsilon},f^{\epsilon}\right)\right\rangle,\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\frac{d}{dt}\left(G^{\epsilon},\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)-\epsilon^{2}\left(G^{\epsilon},\partial_{t}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu}}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(G^{\epsilon}(t),\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\right)+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right\|^{2}+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left[a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}\pm a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-},c^{f^{\epsilon}}\right]\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu}}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1
, one also has from Lemma
5.2
that

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}=\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\epsilon\partial_{t}\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\nabla_{x}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)(t)\right\}+\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathbb{A}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\cdot q_{1}\right)\right\},\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle-\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{\epsilon}(t)\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)(t)+\left\langle[v,-v]\mu^{1/2},Lf^{\epsilon}(t)+\Gamma\left(f^{\epsilon}(t),f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)\right\rangle\right\},\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle-\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\left\{2\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}(t)\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)+2\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}(t)\times\mathfrak{B}\right\},\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)-\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{t}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)(t)\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}b^{f^{\epsilon}}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)-\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)-\epsilon G^{\epsilon}(t)\right\}\right)+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)(t)\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}b^{f^{\epsilon}}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left(a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}\right)(t)\right\|_{\dot{H}^{1}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\min\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t)\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2},\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}G^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-e_{i}}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}b^{f^{\epsilon}}\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Consequently

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

(3.6)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}(t)-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

For
\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}
, since

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\partial_{t}E^{\epsilon}(t)+\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)-\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\partial_{t}\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\eta\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t)\right\|^{2},

we can deduce from Lemma
5.2
that

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right)+\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

(3.7)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\left\|\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}.

For sufficiently small
\kappa>0
,
(\ref{E-R})
+
\kappa\times
(
3.7
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}G_{E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)+\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[E^{\epsilon}(t),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}(t)-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\mathcal{E}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{N,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Here we have set

G_{E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)=-\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}{G^{\epsilon}}(t),\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right)-\kappa\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left(\epsilon\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{2}\partial^{\alpha}\nabla\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right).

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.3
.
∎

Combing Lemma
3.2
with Lemma
3.3
, we can get by taking
N=3
that

Lemma 3.4
.

There exists
G_{f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)
satisfying

G_{f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}

such that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}G_{f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{+}(t)-a^{f^{\epsilon}}_{-}(t)\right\|^{2}

(3.8)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{\epsilon}(t)+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}(t)\times\mathfrak{B}\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq 2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[E^{\epsilon}(t),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq 3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

3.2.
A key estimate related to the electric field
E^{\epsilon}(t,x)

The estimate (
3.8
) tells us that the dissipative effect of the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{\epsilon}(t,x),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
is degenerate for small
\epsilon=\frac{1}{c}
. As pointed out in the introduction, such a degeneracy will lead to a difficulty in dealing with the term

\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left(E^{\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}q_{1}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\right)

and the main purpose of this subsection is to provide another way to bound such a term.
To this end, we need the following result.

Lemma 3.5
.

It holds that

\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{E^{\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}-q_{0}\epsilon(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}\right\}=\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}.

(3.9)

Proof.

From the definition of
{\bf P}
, one has

\displaystyle\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{E^{\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E^{\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}-{\bf P}\left\{E^{\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\}

(3.10)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E^{\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}.

Similarly, one also has

\displaystyle\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{-q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}\right\}

(3.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{-(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\left\{b^{f^{\epsilon}}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}\right\}q_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{-(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot b^{f^{\epsilon}}\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{(\mathfrak{B}\times v)\cdot b^{f^{\epsilon}}\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\{{\bf I-P}\}\left\{\left(b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right)\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right)\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}-{\bf P}\left\{\left(b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right)\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right)\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}.

Therefore, one can get (
3.9
) from (
3.10
) and (
3.11
).
∎

Having obtained Lemma
3.5
, we can get that

Lemma 3.6
.

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 3
with
|\beta|\geq 1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left\{q_{1}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}\right)

(3.12)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i},\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Here

C_{\beta}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left\{\partial_{\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right\}^{2}dv
is some positive constant.

Proof.

Applying
{\bf I-P}
to (
2.6
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}-\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}+{L}f^{\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}

(3.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\epsilon\{{\bf I-P}\}q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}+{\bf P}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}\}+\{{\bf I-P}\}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}}

with

\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}\equiv\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{\epsilon}\cdot vf^{\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}-\epsilon q_{0}\left(v\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}+{\Gamma}\left(f^{\epsilon},f^{\epsilon}\right).

(3.14)

Multiplying (
3.13
) by
\partial_{2\beta}\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\}
and
integrating the resulting identity with respect to
v
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle+\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

(3.15)

\displaystyle\mp\underbrace{\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\rangle}_{J_{1}}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),{\bf P}_{\pm}\left\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}\right\}-{L}_{\pm}f^{\epsilon}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}_{\pm}f^{\epsilon}-\epsilon\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}}\right\rangle.

Noticing that

J_{1}=(-1)^{|\beta|}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left\{\partial_{\beta}(v_{i}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}})\right\}^{2}dv\equiv(-1)^{|\beta|}C_{\beta},

we can get from (
3.9
) that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{\epsilon}-\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle-2(-1)^{|\beta|}C_{\beta}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i}

(3.16)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{\epsilon}-\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),{\bf P}_{+}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}\}-{\bf P}_{-}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}\}-{L}_{+}f^{\epsilon}+-{L}_{-}f^{\epsilon}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}_{+}f^{\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}_{-}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),-\epsilon\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}+\epsilon\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}}-\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}}\right\rangle.

Applying
\partial^{\alpha}
to (
3.16
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

(3.17)

\displaystyle-2(-1)^{|\beta|}C_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{+}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}\}-\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{-}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\partial^{\alpha}{L}_{+}f^{\epsilon}+-\partial^{\alpha}{L}_{-}f^{\epsilon}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{+}f^{\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{-}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),-\epsilon\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}+\epsilon\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}q_{0}(v\times\mathfrak{B})\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}}\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle R^{\epsilon}_{1},

where we use
R^{\epsilon}_{1}
to denote all terms in the right hand side of (
3.17
).

Therefore, one can finally get from Lemma
5.2
that

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i},\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2C_{\beta}}\left(\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle-R^{\epsilon}_{1},\right.

\displaystyle\quad\quad\left.\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C_{\beta}}\left(R_{1}^{\epsilon},\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Thus we have completed the proof of this lemma.
∎

3.3.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

With the above results in hand, we now turn to deduce the key
a priori
estimates on the solution
\left[f^{\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{\epsilon}(t,x),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
of the Cauchy problem (
2.3
), (
2.4
), (
2.5
).

Lemma 3.7
.

It holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

(3.18)

Proof.

For the brevity of the presentation, the proof of this lemma is divided into the following two steps:

Step 1:
Firstly, multiplying (
2.6
) by
f^{\epsilon}
and integrating the resulting identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left[f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(3.19)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\left(q_{0}E^{\epsilon}(t)\cdot vf^{\epsilon}(t),f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{1}}+\underbrace{\left({\Gamma}\left(f^{\epsilon}(t),f^{\epsilon}(t)\right),f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{2}}.

For
I_{1}
, one can easily get from Sobolev’s inequality that

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t),

while by employing the estimate (
5.4
) on the nonlinear collision operator
\Gamma_{\pm}(g_{1},g_{2})
obtained in Lemma
5.2
, one can deduce that

\displaystyle I_{2}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Plugging the above estimates on
I_{1}\sim I_{2}
into (
3.19
), one arrives at

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left[f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(3.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{2,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Next applying
\partial^{\alpha}
with
1\leq|\alpha|\leq 3
to (
2.6
), multiplying the resulting identity by
\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}
, one has by integrating the final result with respect to
v
and
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{0}E^{\epsilon}(t)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{3}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2}\left(\partial\left\{q_{0}E^{\epsilon}(t)\cdot vf^{\epsilon}(t)\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{4}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\epsilon q_{0}v\times\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{5}}+\underbrace{\left(\partial^{\alpha}{\Gamma}\left(f^{\epsilon}(t),f^{\epsilon}(t)\right),\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{6}}.

For
I_{3}
, one has from Sobolev’s inequality that

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{e_{i}}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-e_{i}}_{e_{i}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{2e_{i}}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-2e_{i}}_{e_{i}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|\left\|\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{e_{i}}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-e_{i}}_{e_{i}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{e_{i}}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{3}_{x}}^{2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha-2e_{i}}_{e_{i}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{6}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}E^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}\left\|\nabla_{v}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Similarly, one can also get from Lemma
5.2
and Sobolev’s inequality that

I_{4}+I_{5}+I_{6}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Substituting the above estimates on
I_{3}\sim I_{6}
into (
3.3
) and by exploiting the estimate (
3.20
), one has

\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[f^{\epsilon},E^{\epsilon},\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

(3.21)

holds for
1\leq|\alpha|\leq 3
.

Step 2:
Applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
to (
3.13
), multiplying the result by
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}
with
|\beta|\geq 1
and integrating the final resulting identity with respect to
v
and
x
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(3.22)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+C_{\eta}\left\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{7}}+\underbrace{\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\tilde{G}^{\epsilon}(t)},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right)}_{I_{8}}.

By employing a similar argument used to estimate
I_{5}
and
I_{6}
, one has

I_{7}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

To control
I_{8}
, one has by (
3.12
) and Lemma
5.2
that

\displaystyle I_{8}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{2\beta}\left\{\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i}\cdot v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}_{+}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}_{-}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i}\cdot\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}_{+}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}_{-}(t)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{\epsilon}}\times\mathfrak{B}\right\}_{i},\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}_{+}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{\epsilon}_{-}(t)\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t).

Plugging the estimates on
I_{7}
and
I_{8}
into (
3.22
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(3.23)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+C_{\eta}\left\|\partial^{\alpha+e_{i}}_{\beta-e_{i}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}.

Taking the summation over

\{|\beta|=m,|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 3\}
for each given
1\leq m\leq 3
, and then taking the proper linear
combination of those estimates with properly chosen constants, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 3,|\beta|\geq 1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 3,|\beta|\geq 1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(3.24)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\alpha^{\prime}\leq 3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{2}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{2\beta}\left\{v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}.

A proper linear combination of (
3.8
), (
3.20
), (
3.21
) and (
3.24
) yields (
3.18
). This completes the proof of Lemma 3.7.
∎

3.4.
The proof of Theorem
2.1

Now we are ready to deduce the desired
a priori
estimates on the solution
\left[f^{\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{\epsilon}(t,x),\right.

\left.\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
of the Cauchy problem (
2.3
), (
2.4
), (
2.5
) defined on the strip
\Pi_{T}=[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and complete the proof of Theorem
2.1
.

Suppose that the Cauchy problem (
2.3
), (
2.4
), (
2.5
) admits a unique solution
\left[f^{\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{\epsilon}(t,x),\right.

\left.\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
on the strip
\Pi_{T}=[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
and satisfy the following
a priori assumption

X(t)=\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 3}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\left\|\left[E^{\epsilon}(t),\widetilde{B}^{\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{3}_{x}}\leq M,\quad 0\leq t\leq T

(3.25)

for some sufficiently small positive constant
M>0
.

Under the
a priori
estimates (
3.25
), from (
3.18
), integrating from
0
to
t
, one has for all
0\leq t\leq T
that

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(s)\right\}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{3,f^{\epsilon}}(0),

(3.26)

from which one can deduce (
2.17
) immediately.

Having obtained the estimate (
3.26
), one can then get the global solvability result for the Cauchy problem (
2.3
), (
2.4
), (
2.5
) by the continuation argument and as a by-product of such an argument, one can show that the estimate (
3.26
) holds for all
t\in\mathbb{R}^{+}
. This completes the proof of Theorem
2.1
.

4.
The proof of Theorem
2.2

This section is devoted to proving Theorem
2.2
. To this end, suppose that
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),{B}^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
is a unique solution of the Cauchy problem (
2.42
)-(
2.45
) and for
i=1,2,\cdots,m-1
, let
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
,
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
, and
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
be solutions of the Cauchy problems (
2.36
)-(
2.37
), (
2.38
)-(
2.41
), and (
2.42
)-(
2.45
), respectively, defined on the strip
\Pi_{T}=[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
for some positive constant
T>0
, we now turn to deduce certain energy type
a priori
estimates on
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),\right.

\left.B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
in terms of its initial data in the coming subsections. The first result is on the macro-structure of
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
.

4.1.
Macro-structure for
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]

In this section, the first result is concerned with the dissipative effect of the macroscopic quantities such as
{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
,
a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}(t,x)-a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}(t,x)
, etc. To do so, we first need the following identities concerning the macroscopic part of
f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
.

Lemma 4.1
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(\frac{a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}}{2}\right)+\nabla_{x}\cdot b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}=0,

(4.1)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{j}+\partial_{j}\left(\frac{a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}}{2}+2c^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\right)+\sum\limits_{k=1}^{3}\frac{\partial_{k}\mathbb{A}_{jk}(\{{\bf I-P}\}{f^{m,\epsilon}}\cdot[1,1])}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{a^{f^{P,\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{P,\epsilon}}_{-}}{2}E^{m,\epsilon}_{j}+\frac{a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}}{2}E^{P,\epsilon}_{j}+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\frac{a^{f^{j_{2},\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{j_{2},\epsilon}}_{-}}{2}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}_{j}

\displaystyle+\epsilon\left[G^{i,\epsilon}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right]_{j},\quad j=1,2,3,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}c^{f^{m,\epsilon}}+\frac{1}{3}\nabla_{x}\cdot b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}+\frac{5}{6}\sum\limits_{j=1}^{3}\partial_{j}\mathbb{B}_{j}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}{f^{i,\epsilon}}\cdot[1,1]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{6}G^{P,\epsilon}\cdot E^{m,\epsilon}+\frac{1}{6}G^{m,\epsilon}\cdot E^{P,\epsilon}+\frac{1}{6}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}G^{j_{2},\epsilon}\cdot E^{j_{1},\epsilon},

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}\right)+\nabla_{x}\cdot G^{m,\epsilon}=0,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}G^{m,\epsilon}+\nabla_{x}\left(a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}-a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}\right)-2E^{m,\epsilon}+\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathbb{A}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}{f^{m,\epsilon}}\cdot q_{1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E^{P,\epsilon}\left(a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}\right)+E^{m,\epsilon}\left(a^{f^{P,\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{P,\epsilon}}_{-}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\left(a^{f^{j_{2},\epsilon}}_{+}+a^{f^{j_{2},\epsilon}}_{-}\right)+2\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\langle[v,-v]\mu^{1/2},-{L}f^{m,\epsilon}+{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)\right\rangle,

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\mathbb{A}_{jk}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}{f^{m,\epsilon}}\cdot[1,1]\right)+\partial_{k}b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{j}+\partial_{j}b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{k}-\frac{2}{3}\delta_{jk}\nabla_{x}\cdot b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}-\frac{5}{3}\delta_{jk}\nabla_{x}\cdot\mathbb{B}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}{f^{m,\epsilon}}\cdot[1,1]\right)

(4.2)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{A}_{jk}\left(r^{m,\epsilon}_{+}+r^{m,\epsilon}_{-}+g^{m,\epsilon}_{+}+g^{m,\epsilon}_{-}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\mathbb{B}_{k}\left(\{{\bf I-P}\}{f^{m,\epsilon}}\cdot[1,1]\right)+\partial_{k}c^{f^{m,\epsilon}}=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{B}_{k}\left(r^{m,\epsilon}_{+}+r^{m,\epsilon}_{-}+g^{m,\epsilon}_{+}+g^{m,\epsilon}_{-}\right),

where

\displaystyle r^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-v\cdot\nabla_{x}\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-{L}_{\pm}f^{m,\epsilon},

\displaystyle g^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\pm\frac{1}{2}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}_{\pm}\pm\frac{1}{2}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}

(4.3)

\displaystyle\pm\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{2},\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{j_{2},\epsilon}_{\pm}\pm\frac{1}{2}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle\mp\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}\pm\frac{1}{2}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{1},\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{j_{1},\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle\mp\epsilon\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\left\{f^{P,\epsilon}_{\pm}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}f^{i,\epsilon}_{\pm}\right\}\mp\epsilon\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}B^{i}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle\pm\frac{\epsilon^{m}}{2}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}\mp\epsilon^{m+1}\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}_{\pm}

\displaystyle+{\Gamma}_{\pm}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}_{\pm}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\epsilon^{m}{\Gamma}_{\pm}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma_{\pm}\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right),

\displaystyle G^{j,\epsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle v\mu^{1/2},\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{j,\epsilon}\cdot q_{1}\right\rangle,\quad j=1,2,\cdots,m.

Based on the above lemma, one has by repeating the argument used in the proof of
[
23
, Lemma 3.2]
that

Lemma 4.2
.

There exists
G_{f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}}(t)
satisfying

G_{f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}}(t)\lesssim\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}

such that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}G_{f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}

(4.4)

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}(t)\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j}B^{j}\right\}\right\|^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha|\leq N-1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N}(t)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}\leq m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N}(t)

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

4.2.
Some estimates related to the electromagnetic field and the transport term

The main purpose of this section is to derive certain estimates related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
and the linear transport term
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
. As pointed out before, compared with the problem on global solvability near Maxwellians for fixed light speed studied in
[
5
]
, the difficulty we encountered here is due to the degeneracy of the dissipative effect of the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
for large light speed, cf. the small parameter
\epsilon
in front of terms related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
in the definitions of the
energy dissipation rate functionals
\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)
,
\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,\ell,-\gamma}(t)
, and
\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,\ell,-\gamma}(t)
given by (
2.1.2
).
To deduce an
\epsilon-
independent estimates on
f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
, unlike
[
5
]
, we had to use a different way to deal with all those terms related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
. Moreover, since weighted energy estimates will be used, we also need to control the terms related to the linear transport term
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}(t,,x,v)
suitably.

4.2.1.
Some linear estimates related to the electromegnetic field and the transport term

This section focuses on dealing with some estimates on some linear terms related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
and the linear transport term
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}(t,,x,v)
.

Our first result is on some linear terms related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
.

Lemma 4.3
.

We have the following estimates:

(i).

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N
with
|\beta|\geq 1
or
|\alpha|=|\beta|=0
,
it holds that

\displaystyle\left(\partial_{\beta}^{\alpha}\left\{q_{1}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\{\bf I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

(4.5)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\sum\limits_{i=1}^{3}\left(\left\{\partial^{\alpha}E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}_{i},\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N}(t)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}\leq m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N}(t).

Here

C_{\beta}(t)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left\{w_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left(v_{i}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right\}^{2}dv

depends only on the time variable
t
but satisfies
C_{\beta}\sim 1
for
t\geq 0
;

(ii).

For
|\alpha|\leq N-1
, it also holds that

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{1}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

(4.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{3}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}_{i},\left\langle\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f_{m,+}-\partial^{\alpha}f_{m,-}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4{C_{0}}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\left\|\left\langle\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f_{m,+}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}f_{m,-}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N}(t)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}\leq m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N}(t).

Here again

C_{0}(t)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left\{w_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\left(v_{i}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\right\}^{2}dv
depends only on
t
and satisfies
C_{0}(t)\sim 1
.

Proof.

To prove Lemma
4.3
, we can get first the following equation for
{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}
by applying
{\bf\{I-P\}}
to (
2.38
):

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}

(4.7)

\displaystyle+v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f-{\bf P}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}f)+{L}f^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right)-{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)-{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\epsilon\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\left\{f^{P,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}f^{i,\epsilon}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)-{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(\frac{\epsilon^{m}}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+\Gamma\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)\right\}+\epsilon^{m}{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\left[I^{m,\epsilon,+}_{mic,mix}(t),I^{m,\epsilon,-}_{mic,mix}(t)\right],

where we
[I^{m,\epsilon,+}_{mic,mix}(t),I^{m,\epsilon,-}_{mic,mix}(t)]
to denote the all terms in the right-hand side of the above vector equality for brevity.

Multiplying (
4.7
) by
\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\}
and integrating the resulting identity with respect to
v
over
\mathbb{R}^{3}
, one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle+\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

(4.8)

\displaystyle\mp\underbrace{\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right\rangle}_{{J}_{1}}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},{\bf P}_{\pm}\left\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\}-{L}_{\pm}f^{m,\epsilon}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}_{\pm}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},-\epsilon{\bf\{I_{\pm}-P_{\pm}\}}q_{0}\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},I^{m,\epsilon,\pm}_{mic,mix}(t)\right\rangle.

Noticing that

{J}_{1}=(-1)^{|\beta|}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left\{w_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}(v_{i}\mu^{\frac{1}{2}})\right\}^{2}dv\equiv(-1)^{|\beta|}{C}_{\beta},

we can get from (
4.8
) that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

(4.9)

\displaystyle-2(-1)^{|\beta|}C_{\beta}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\{{\bf P}_{+}-{\bf P}_{-}\}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\}-\{{L}_{+}-L_{-}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}_{+}f^{m,\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}_{-}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},-\epsilon{\bf\{I_{+}-P_{+}\}}q_{0}\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\epsilon{\bf\{I_{-}-P_{-}\}}q_{0}\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},I^{m,\epsilon,+}_{mic,mix}(t)-I^{m,\epsilon,-}_{mic,mix}(t)\right\rangle.

Applying
\partial^{\alpha}
to (
4.9
), one has

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

(4.10)

\displaystyle-2(-1)^{|\beta|}C_{\beta}\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{t}\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\nabla_{x}\cdot\left\langle v\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{+}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\}-\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{-}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\partial^{\alpha}{L}_{+}f^{m,\epsilon}+\partial^{\alpha}{L}_{-}f^{m,\epsilon}-v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{+}f^{m,\epsilon}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf P}_{-}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},-\epsilon{\bf\{I_{+}-P_{+}\}}q_{0}\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\epsilon{\bf\{I_{-}-P_{-}\}}q_{0}\left\{v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}I^{m,\epsilon,+}_{mic,mix}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}I^{m,\epsilon,-}_{mic,mix}(t)\right\rangle

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle\mathcal{{RHS}}_{1},

where we use
\mathcal{{RHS}}_{1}
to denote all terms in the right hand side of (
4.10
).

Therefore, we can get from Lemma
5.2
, Lemma
5.3
, and Lemma
5.4
that

\displaystyle(-1)^{|\beta|}\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}_{i},\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2C_{\beta}}\left(\partial_{t}\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle-\mathcal{RHS}_{1},\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\left.\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2C_{\beta}}\left(\mathcal{RHS}_{1},\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{\ell-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\left\|\nabla^{|\alpha|+1}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N}(t)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}\leq m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N}(t),

which is (
4.5
),
and (
4.6
) can be obtained in a similar way. Thus we have completed the proof of this lemma.
∎

For the corresponding term related to the linear transport term
v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
, we have the following result.

Lemma 4.4
.

Assume
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{m}+1
with
|\beta|\geq 1
,and

\sigma_{n,k}-\sigma_{n,k-1}=\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta),\ \ 1\leq k\leq n.

(4.11)

one has

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}),w^{2}_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

(4.12)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|-1}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,|\beta|-1)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

By Cauchy’s inequality, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}(v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}),w^{2}_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-1}\right\|^{2}+\eta\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

To control
\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-1}\right\|^{2}
, one applies different time-rates
factors to deduce

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}-1}\right\|^{2}

(4.13)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|-1}-\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta)}\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\right\|^{\frac{4(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{\frac{-2\gamma-8}{\gamma-2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|=n}\left\{(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|-1}-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\right\|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|-1}}\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta-e_{j}|,1}\partial^{\alpha+e_{j}}_{\beta-e_{j}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}\right\},

where we used (
4.11
),
then one has (
4.12
) by (
4.13
).
∎

4.2.2.
Some estimates on the interactions between
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
,
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
, and
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]

(i=1,2,3,\cdots,m-1)

In this subsection, we will focus on some estimates on the interactions between
\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
,
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
, and
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]

(i=1,\cdots,m-1)
, in which the electromagnetic
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
are involved, with respect to the weight
w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}(t,v)
for both
\kappa=-\gamma
and
\kappa=1
.

First for estimates on the interactions between
E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)
and
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)\right]
with respect to the weight
w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}(t,v)
for both
\kappa=-\gamma
and
\kappa=1
, we have the following result.

Lemma 4.5
.

Take
n\geq 2
and
w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}(t,v)=\langle v\rangle^{\kappa(l_{m}-|\beta|)}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}
, one has the following estimates:

i)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{n}_{x}}^{2}\left\|\nabla_{x}f^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{n-1}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu}}^{2}+\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{n}_{x}}^{2}\left\|{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{P,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}_{H^{n}_{x}}+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,n+1-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t).

ii)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.15)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+1+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m},\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

iii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n
with
|\beta|\geq 1
or
|\alpha|=|\beta|=0
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(\frac{\epsilon}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.16)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+1+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

We only prove (
4.16
) for simplicity. For this purpose, we can deduce from the triangle inequality that

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\left(\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot v{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{P,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{I_{\alpha,\beta,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot v{\bf P}f^{P,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{I_{\alpha,\beta,2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf P}\left(\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{I_{\alpha,\beta,3}}.

I_{\alpha,\beta,1}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle I_{\alpha,\beta,1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left(\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot v\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{P,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\left(\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\partial_{e_{j}}v\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta-e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{P,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{0<\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}\left|\left(\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot v\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{P,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|.

By
L^{2}-L^{\infty}-L^{2}
,
L^{2}-L^{\infty}-L^{2}
,
L^{3}-L^{6}-L^{2}
or
L^{6}-L^{3}-L^{2}
type Sobolev inequalities, one can deduce

\displaystyle I_{\alpha,\beta,1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n,\atop|\beta|\geq 1}\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{n-2}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{P,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+1+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

For
I_{\alpha,\beta,2}
and
I_{\alpha,\beta,3}
, one has

\displaystyle I_{\alpha,\beta,2}+I_{\alpha,\beta,3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{n-2}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{P,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{n-1}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu}}+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

Thus (
4.16
) follows by collecting the estimates on
I_{\alpha,\beta,1}
,
I_{\alpha,\beta,2}
and
I_{\alpha,\beta,3}
into (
4.17
).
∎

Similarly, one can also deduce the following estimates on the interactions between
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
with
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),f^{1,\epsilon}(t,x,v),\cdots,f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)\right]
with respect to the weight
w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}(t,v)
for both
\kappa=-\gamma
and
\kappa=1
:

Lemma 4.6
.

Take
n\geq 2
and
w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}=\langle v\rangle^{\kappa(l_{i}-|\beta|)}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}
, one has the following estimates:

i)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
and
0<j_{1}<m
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot f^{j_{1},\epsilon}-q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{{j_{1}},\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.18)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)

and one also has for
j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m
with
0<j_{1},j_{2}<m
that

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{2},\epsilon}-q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\nabla_{v}f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right.\right.

(4.19)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-q_{0}\epsilon\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\left\{f^{P,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}f^{i,\epsilon}\right\}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t).

ii)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
with
0<j_{1}<m
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot f^{j_{1},\epsilon}-q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{{j_{1}},\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

(4.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m},\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

and one also has for
j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m
with
0<j_{1},j_{2}<m
that

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{2},\epsilon}-q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\nabla_{v}f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right.\right.

(4.21)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-q_{0}\epsilon\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\left(f^{P,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}f^{i,\epsilon}\right)\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m},\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

iii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n,\ |\beta|\geq 1
or
\alpha=\beta=0
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot f^{j_{1},\epsilon}-q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{{j_{1}},\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}\right\}\right\},\right.

(4.22)

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n+1,n+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

and one also has for
j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m
with
0<j_{1},j_{2}<m
that

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left[\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{2},\epsilon}-q_{0}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\nabla_{v}f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right.\right.\right.\right.

(4.23)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad-q_{0}\epsilon\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\left(f^{P,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}f^{i,\epsilon}\right)\right]\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n+1,-\frac{\kappa l_{m}}{\gamma}+n+2+\frac{\kappa n-1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

4.2.3.
Some nonlinear estimates related to the electromagnetic field with respect to the weight
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)

In this section, we try to deduce certain weighted nonlinear estimates related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
with respect to the weight
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
. For result in this direction, we have

Lemma 4.7
.

Recall the definitions in (
2.1.2
),
take

n\geq 3,\ \widetilde{\ell}_{m}\geq 1-\frac{3\gamma}{2},\ {\theta}=\frac{2-4\gamma}{2\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma},

we have the following estimates:

i)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\epsilon^{m}q_{0}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.24)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},3,4-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\|\epsilon^{m}\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{n-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.25)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},3,4-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla^{n}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t);

ii)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\epsilon^{m}q_{0}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.26)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\epsilon^{m}\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}
