\displaystyle+\left\{\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\sum_{1\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}^{(j,1)}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\epsilon^{m}\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\{\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\sum_{0\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}^{(j,0)}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t);

iii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|=n
with
|\beta|\geq 1
or
|\alpha|=|\beta|=0
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(\epsilon^{m}q_{0}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\epsilon^{m}\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\beta|\geq 1}\left\{\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\sum_{|\beta|\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\},\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.29)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\epsilon^{m}\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\beta|\geq 1}\left\{\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\sum_{|\beta|\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\},\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t).

Proof.

We only prove (
4.26
) since the rest can be done similarly. To this end, since

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\epsilon^{m}q_{0}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

(4.30)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)}_{{J}_{\alpha,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{0<\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)}_{{J}_{\alpha,2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{0<\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)}_{{J}_{\alpha,3}},

we can get by using integration by parts that

\displaystyle{J}_{\alpha,1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{m}\left\{\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\right\|^{2}.

For
{J}_{\alpha,2}
, one has by Sobolev’s inequalities that

{J}_{\alpha,2}\lesssim\epsilon^{m}\left\{\left\|E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{H^{n}_{x}}+\|\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}\|_{H^{n}_{x}}\right\}\|\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{n-2}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu}}+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and
{J}_{\alpha,3}
can be estimated as follows

\displaystyle{J}_{\alpha,3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underbrace{\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}}_{{J}^{(1)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N^{0}_{R}-1}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{6}_{x}}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{6}_{x}}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}L^{3}_{x}}}_{{J}^{(2)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N^{0}_{R}}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}}_{{J}^{(3)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N^{0}_{R}+1}\underbrace{\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{N^{0}_{R}+1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N_{m}-2}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left[E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}}_{{J}^{(4)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N_{R}-1}\underbrace{\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{m}-1}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{2}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}^{2}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{3}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}}_{{J}^{(5)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|=N_{R}}\underbrace{\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N_{m}}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{3\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}L^{2}_{v}}}_{{J}^{(6)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

To estimate
{J}^{(i)}_{\alpha,3}

(i=1,2,3,4,5,6)
, one has for
{J}^{(1)}_{\alpha,3}
that

\displaystyle{J}^{(1)}_{\alpha,3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\epsilon^{2m}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\widetilde{\ell}_{m}}\right\|^{2{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2(1-{\theta})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\epsilon^{2m}\left\{\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\widetilde{\ell}_{m}}\right\|^{2{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2(1-{\theta})}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\{\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\widetilde{\ell}_{m}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-1,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\{\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\epsilon\partial^{\alpha_{1}}B^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{1\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}^{(j,1)}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t),

where
{\theta}
satisfies that

1-\frac{3}{2}\gamma=\frac{\gamma}{2}(1-{\theta})+\widetilde{\ell}_{m}{\theta}
which yields that
{\theta}=\frac{2-4\gamma}{2\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma}
.

{J}^{(2)}_{\alpha,3}
and
{J}^{(3)}_{\alpha,3}
can be controlled in a similar way, while for the last three terms, one can deduce that

\displaystyle{J}^{(4)}_{\alpha,3}+{J}^{(5)}_{\alpha,3}+{J}^{(6)}_{\alpha,3}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t).

Thus we complete the proof of this lemma.
∎

For the estimates on the interactions of
f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
with
\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x),E^{1,\epsilon}(t,x),\cdots,E^{m-1,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
with respect to the weight
w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
, we can get similarly that

Lemma 4.8
.

Assume

\widetilde{\ell}_{m}\geq 1-\frac{3\gamma}{2},\ {\theta}=\frac{2-4\gamma}{2\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma},

we have the following estimates:

i)

For
0\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.31)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{n-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla^{n}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)

and for
0<j_{1}<m
, one can get that

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.32)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)

\displaystyle+\|\nabla_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{n-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\|E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla^{n}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t).

ii)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.33)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{{\theta}}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1,\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,-\gamma l_{m}+\widetilde{l}_{m},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

and we also have

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.34)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{{\theta}}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1,\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,-\gamma l_{m}+\widetilde{l}_{m},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t).

iii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n
with
|\beta|\geq 1
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.35)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{{\theta}}}\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\},\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,-\gamma l_{m}+\widetilde{l}_{m},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

and we also have

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\}\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.36)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{{\theta}}}\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\},\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,-\gamma l_{m}+\widetilde{l}_{m},1}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t).

4.2.4.
Some nonlinear estimates related to the electromagnetic field with respect to the weight
w_{\ell-|\beta|,1}(t,v)

As we mentioned above, two sets of energy estimates with respect to weights
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
and
w_{\ell-|\beta|,1}(t,v)
respectively are introduced to close the desired energy estimates, the main purpose of this section is to deduce certain nonlinear estimates related to the electromagnetic field
\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(tx)\right]
with respect to the weight
w_{\ell-|\beta|,1}(t,v)
.

Lemma 4.9
.

For
2\leq n\leq N_{m}+1
, we have the following estimates:

i)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{0}\epsilon^{m}(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.37)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{1\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,1)}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\|\nabla^{N^{0}_{R}-1}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,1)}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.38)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,1)}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\|\nabla^{N^{0}_{R}-1}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},0,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,1)}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2};

ii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left\{q_{0}\epsilon^{m}(E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\beta|\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\}\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\|\epsilon\nabla^{N^{0}_{R}-1}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\beta|\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\}\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.40)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\partial^{\alpha_{1}}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\|\epsilon\nabla^{N^{0}_{R}-1}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}}^{2}\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

To simplify the presentation, we only prove (
4.37
) since the other estimates can be obtained similarly. For this purpose, since

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

(4.41)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)}_{\tilde{H}_{\alpha,1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{0<\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)}_{\tilde{H}_{\alpha,2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{0<\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)}_{\tilde{H}_{\alpha,3}},

we can get by using integrations by parts and Holder’s inequality that

\tilde{H}_{\alpha,1}\lesssim\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and

\tilde{H}_{\alpha,2}\lesssim\left\|\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}+1}_{x}}\|\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

For
\tilde{H}_{\alpha,3}
, one has

\displaystyle\tilde{H}_{\alpha,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{0<\alpha_{1}\leq\alpha}\left(q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-1,1}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|}_{\tilde{H}^{(1)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N^{0}_{m}-1}\left\|\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{L^{6}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-1,1}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{3}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|}_{\tilde{H}^{(2)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|=N^{0}_{m}}\left\|\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-1,1}\partial_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|_{L^{2}_{v}L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|}_{\tilde{H}^{(3)}_{\alpha,3}}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}

\displaystyle\times\underbrace{\sum_{|\alpha_{1}|\geq N^{0}_{m}+1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left|\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right|\left|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-1,1}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|\left|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right|dvdx}_{\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{\alpha,3}}.

To control the terms
\tilde{H}^{(j)}_{\alpha,3}
for
j=1,2,3,4
, we first deal with

\tilde{H}^{(3)}_{\alpha,3}
as follows

\displaystyle\tilde{H}^{(3)}_{\alpha,3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-1,1}\partial^{\alpha-\alpha_{1}}_{e_{j}}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq|\alpha_{1}|\leq N^{0}_{R}-2}\left\|\epsilon^{m}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{1\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,1)}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and
\tilde{H}^{(1)}_{\alpha,3}
and
\tilde{H}^{(2)}_{\alpha,3}
can be estimated similarly.

As for
\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{\alpha,3}
,
one has by Sobolev’s inequalities that

\tilde{H}^{(4)}_{\alpha,3}\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

Collecting the above estimates, one can deduce (
4.37
) immediately.
∎

Similarly, for the estimates on the interactions of
f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
with
\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x),E^{1,\epsilon}(t,x),\cdots,E^{m-1,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
with respect to the weight
w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}(t,v)
, we also have the following lemma, whose proofs will be omitted for brevity.

Lemma 4.10
.

For
2\leq n\leq N_{m}+1
, we have the following estimates:

i)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.42)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-2}_{x}}\right\}\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1,\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.43)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-2}_{x}}\right\}\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2};

ii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n
with
|\beta|\geq 1
or
|\alpha|=|\beta|=0
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.44)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-2}_{x}}\right\}\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\}\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{i,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\bf\{I-P\}}\left(q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}-\frac{1}{2}q_{0}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\left\|E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-2}_{x}}\right\}\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\}\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{i,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{\ell^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

4.3.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)
,
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)
, and
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

Now we are going to derive certain Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)
,
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)
, and
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)
. Our first result is to deduce the Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)
.

4.3.1.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

For result on this problem, we can get that

Lemma 4.11
.

Take

\widetilde{\ell}_{m}\geq 1-\frac{3\gamma}{2},\ {\theta}=\frac{2-4\gamma}{2\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma},

and assume that

\displaystyle\max

\displaystyle\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}(1+\tau)^{1+\vartheta}\left\|\left[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\right](\tau)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(\tau),\right.

(4.46)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \left.\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}

is sufficiently small,
then one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

(4.47)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq N_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t)+\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

Proof.

Step 1:

with

), multiplying the resulting identity by

(4.48)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}-1,N_{m}-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},3,4-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}-1,N_{m}-1,-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}-1,N_{m}-\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[\epsilon E^{j_{1},\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{N_{m}-1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

Step 2:

For simplicity in presentation, we use the notations

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}

(4.49)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{1},\epsilon}-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{j_{1},\epsilon}

\displaystyle-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\epsilon\left(v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}\left\{f^{P,\epsilon}+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{i}f^{i,\epsilon}\right\}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{j_{2},\epsilon}

\displaystyle-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{j_{2},\epsilon}

\displaystyle+{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+\Gamma\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)\right\}

and

\displaystyle I_{pure,mic}(t)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}E^{P,\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}

(4.50)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\sum_{0<j_{1}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}}E^{j_{1},\epsilon}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\epsilon^{m}pE^{m,\epsilon}\cdot vf^{m,\epsilon}-{\bf\{I-P\}}q_{0}\epsilon^{m}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon v\times B^{m,\epsilon}\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{m}\Gamma(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}),

then (
4.7

(4.51)

\displaystyle+\epsilon q_{0}\left\{\left(v\times\left(B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right)\right)\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}+Lf^{m,\epsilon}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle I_{mix,mic}(t)+I_{pure,mic}(t).

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+\underbrace{\left|\left(-{\bf\{I-P\}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{S}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{S}_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(I_{mix,mic}(t),w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{S}_{3}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(I_{pure,mic}(t),w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{S}_{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\epsilon q_{0}\left\{v\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{S}_{5}}.

For
\mathcal{S}_{1}
, one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\left(v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|+\left|\left(v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(
4.5

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\left\{w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}-{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

(4.53)

\displaystyle+\left\|f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\left\|\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},2}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},2}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t).

Using (
4.16
), (
4.22
), (
4.23
), and (

) with

,
one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},3,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

(4.54)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},3,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},3,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},2,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

As for
\mathcal{S}_{4}
, one has from (
4.35
), (
4.36
), (
4.28
), (
4.29
) and (

) with

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim
