\displaystyle+\chi_{|\beta|\geq 1}\left\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon}]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}[E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{|\beta|-1\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,|\alpha|+|\beta|\},\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq N_{m}^{0}+1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta\|f^{m,\epsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t).

A proper linear combination of (
4.4
), (
4.48
), (
4.56
), (
4.3.1
), and (
4.68
) implies that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{m}}\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N_{m}}\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f\|^{2}_{\nu}

(4.69)

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{m}}\left\{\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\|^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon}]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}[E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq N_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma}}(t)+\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2},

where we used the smallness of (
4.46
).
Thus one gets (
4.47
).
∎

Remark 4.1
.

Since
\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}
is absent in the dissipation rate functional
\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)
, thus we attempt to use
\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)
to control
\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}
in the right hand side of (
4.47
).

4.3.2.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

For the estimate of
\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)
, we can get that

Lemma 4.12
.

Assume that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+1,-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}

(4.70)

is sufficiently small, where
1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1
, then one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

(4.71)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{N_{m}}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t),E^{P,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},3,4-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}.

Proof.

In fact, if we replace
N_{m}
in the estimate (
4.48
) by
N_{m}+1
, one can deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N_{m}+1}\left\|\left[\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon},\partial^{\alpha}E^{m,\epsilon},\partial^{\alpha}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}+\sum_{|\alpha|\leq N_{m}+1}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(4.72)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},3,4-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+1,-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\|\nabla_{x}[E^{j_{1},\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}]\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{N_{m}}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\|[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon}]\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|^{2}+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t),

where we use the smallness of (
4.70
).

We can deduce (
4.71
) by a proper linear combination of (
4.72
) and (
4.4
) with
N=N_{m}+1
, thus the proof is complete.
∎

4.4.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell,-\gamma}(t)

To yield the desired Lyapunov-type inequalities for the energy functional
\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell,-\gamma}(t)
, we first need first to perform some energy estimates with negative Sobolev space.

4.4.1.
Energy estimates with negative Sobolev space

By applying similar argument used to prove Lemma 3.2 in
[
23
, page 3727]
and Lemma 3.3 in
[
23
, page 3731]
, one has

Lemma 4.13
.

Let
\varrho\in\left[\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{2}\right)
, there exists an interactive functional
G^{-\varrho}_{f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}}(t)
satisfying

G^{-\varrho}_{f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}}(t)\lesssim\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t),E^{m,\epsilon}(t),B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left[f^{m,\epsilon}(t),E^{m,\epsilon}(t),B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}

(4.73)

such that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}G^{-\varrho}_{f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}}(t)+\left\|\Lambda^{1-\varrho}\left[{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left(a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{+}(t)-a^{f^{m,\epsilon}}_{-}(t)\right)\right\|^{2}

(4.74)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},2}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},2}(t)+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{H^{1}_{x}L^{2}_{\nu}}^{2}

holds for any
0\leq t\leq T
.

Moreover, by applying the argument used to prove Lemma 3.1 in
[
23
, page 3724]
, one has

Lemma 4.14
.

Under the assumptions stated above, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}(f^{m,\epsilon},E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon})\right\|^{2}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\{{\bf I-P}\}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}

(4.75)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\frac{1}{2}\mathfrak{R}\left(\mathcal{F}\left[q_{0}G_{m}\cdot vf^{P,\epsilon}\right]\mid|\xi|^{-2{\varrho}}{\mathcal{F}[{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon}]}\right)-\mathfrak{R}\left(\mathcal{F}[q_{0}G_{m}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{P,\epsilon}]\mid|\xi|^{-2{\varrho}}{\mathcal{F}({\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon})}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\{\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},2,3-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t).

4.4.2.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for
\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell,-\gamma}(t)

By employing the argument used to prove Lemma
4.11
and by applying (
4.74
) and (
4.75
), we can get that

Lemma 4.15
.

Assume that

\displaystyle\max

\displaystyle\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}(1+\tau)^{1+\vartheta}\left\|\left[\epsilon E^{m,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon}\right](\tau)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}},\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}+1,l^{0}_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(\tau),\right.

(4.76)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \left.\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\sum_{0<j<m}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f_{j},N^{0}_{m}+1,l^{0}_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}

is sufficiently small, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)

(4.77)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N^{0}_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l^{0}_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}+1,l^{0}_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}+1,l^{0}_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}+1,l^{0}_{m}+2-\frac{1}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\left\|\nabla^{N^{0}_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|_{\nu}^{2}.

4.5.
Temporal decay rates

To control

\begin{split}&\left\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\\
&\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N^{0}_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l^{0}_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t),\end{split}

(4.78)

\begin{split}&\left\{\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}-1}_{x}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2m}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}\\
&\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq N_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t),\end{split}

(4.79)

\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{N_{m}}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2},

(4.80)

and

\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t,x),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

(4.81)

in the right hand side of (
4.47
), (
4.77
) and (
4.71
), we need the time decay rates property of

\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}^{2},\ \ \left\|\left[E^{m,\epsilon}(t),B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}^{0}}_{x}}.

For result in this direction, we have

Lemma 4.16
.

Assume

•

N_{m}^{0}\geq 5
and
N_{m}+1=2N_{m}^{0}-1+[\varrho]
;

•

N_{m-1}\geq N_{m}+2
,
N_{j}\geq N_{j+1}+1
for
0<j<m-1
,
N_{P}\geq N_{1}+1
;

•

there exist
\widehat{l}_{m}>\frac{N_{P}+\varrho}{2}
, and

\widehat{l}_{m-1}\geq\widehat{l}_{m},\widehat{l}_{j-1}\geq\widehat{l}_{j}
for
2\leq j\leq m-1
,
\widehat{l}^{P}\geq\widehat{l}_{1}

such that if

\displaystyle\Xi_{m}(t)

(4.82)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\max\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},N^{0}_{m}+\widehat{l}_{m},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(\tau),\right.

\displaystyle\left.\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},N_{P}+\widehat{l}_{P},-\gamma}(\tau),\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j},N_{j}+\widehat{l}_{j},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}

is sufficiently small,
then one has for
k=0,1,2,\cdots,N^{0}_{m}-2
that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\lesssim\epsilon^{-2(k+\varrho)}(1+t)^{-(k+\varrho)}\Xi_{R}(t)

(4.83)

holds for
0\leq t\leq T
.

Proof.

First notice that under the smallness assumption imposed on
\Xi_{m}(t)
, by applying similar strategy as Lemma 4.1 in
[
23
, page 3745]
, one can deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\leq 0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T.

(4.84)

Combing with the above inequality, one has by a similar argument that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\leq 0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T.

(4.85)

Now we turn to compare the difference between the energy functionals

\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)

and the dissipation functionals

\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t).

To this end, since

\left\|\nabla^{k}\left[E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|\leq\left\{\left\|\nabla^{k+1}\epsilon\left[E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|\right\}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+\varrho+1}}\left\{\epsilon^{-k-\varrho}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left[E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+\varrho+1}},

\left\|\nabla^{N^{R}_{0}}[E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|\lesssim\left\{\epsilon\left\|\nabla^{N^{R}_{0}-1}[E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|\right\}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+\varrho+1}}\left\{\epsilon^{-k-\varrho}\left\|\nabla^{N^{R}_{0}+k+\varrho}[E^{m,\epsilon},B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+\varrho+1}},

and for
0<j<m

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{k}\left[{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon},{\bf P}f^{P,\epsilon},{\bf P}f^{j,\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j,\epsilon}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\epsilon\left\|\nabla^{k+1}\left[{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon},{\bf P}f^{P,\epsilon},{\bf P}f^{j,\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j,\epsilon}\right]\right\|\right\}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+\varrho+1}}\left\{\epsilon^{-k-\varrho}\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left[{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon},{\bf P}f^{P,\epsilon},{\bf P}f^{j,\epsilon},E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j,\epsilon}\right]\right\|\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+\varrho+1}}

holds for the macroscopic component, while for the microscopic component, we have by employing the H
\ddot{o}
lder inequality that

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left[f^{P,\epsilon},f^{j,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|

(4.86)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left[f^{P,\epsilon},f^{j,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right]\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+\varrho+1}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left[f^{P,\epsilon},f^{j,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right]\langle v\rangle^{-\frac{\gamma(k+\varrho)}{2}}\right\|^{\frac{1}{k+\varrho+1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left[f^{P,\epsilon},f^{j,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|_{\nu}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+\varrho+1}}\left\|w_{\frac{k+\varrho}{2},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}\left[f^{P,\epsilon},f^{j,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right]\right\|^{\frac{1}{k+\varrho+1}}.

Therefore, we arrive at by collecting the interpolation estimates that

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}^{\frac{k+\varrho}{k+\varrho+1}}\times\left\{\Xi_{R}(t)\right\}^{\frac{1}{k+\varrho+1}},

which combing with (
4.5
) yields that

\begin{split}\ &\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}\\
&+\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}^{1+\frac{1}{k+\varrho}}\times\left\{\Xi_{R}(t)\right\}^{-\frac{1}{k+\varrho}}\leq 0.\end{split}

Solving the above inequality directly gives (
4.83
),
this completes the proof of Lemma
4.16
.
∎

Based on the above lemma and by applying Lemma 3.6 of
[
5
]
with straightforward modification, we can further obtain the following temporal decay rates of
\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}^{0},\ell_{m},-\gamma}(t)
.

Lemma 4.17
.

Under the assumption of the above lemma, let
\ell_{m}\geq N_{P}
and

\ell_{m-1}\geq\ell_{m},\ell_{j-1}\geq\ell_{j}
for
2\leq j\leq m-1
,
\ell^{P}\geq\ell_{1}
, furthermore, if

\displaystyle\Xi_{R,weight}(t)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\max\left\{\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},\ell_{m}+\widehat{l}_{m},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},\ell_{m},-\gamma}(\tau),\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(\tau),\right.

\displaystyle\left.\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{P}+\widehat{l}_{P},-\gamma}(\tau),\sum_{1\leq j\leq m-1}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j},\ell_{j}+\widehat{l}_{j},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}

is sufficiently small, then
one has for
k\leq N_{m}^{0}-3
,

\begin{split}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},{\ell_{R}},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{0<j<m}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j},{\ell_{j}},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell^{P},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim&\epsilon^{-2(k+\varrho)}(1+t)^{-(k+\varrho)}\Xi_{R,weight}(t).\end{split}

(4.87)

4.6.
Lyapunov-type inequalities for some weighted estimates with the weight
w_{l_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}(t,v)

Now we are ready to get some Lyapunov-type inequalities for some weighted estimates with the weight
w_{l_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}(t,v)
. It is worth to pointing out that, unlike the estimates obtained in Lemma

4.15
which lead to the increase of the weights in the corresponding terms in the right hand side of the estimate (
4.77
), the estimates we obtained in the coming two lemmas will not lead to the growth of the weights.

Lemma 4.18
.

Assume that

\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{(1+\tau)^{1+\vartheta}\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\left[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}\right](\tau)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right\}

(4.88)

is sufficiently small,
then one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

(4.89)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}\left\{(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop|\alpha|+|\beta|=n}\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}+2+2\vartheta}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-3}_{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,n\},\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+2-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}+1}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}}\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t).

Proof.

Step 1:

Firstly, performing
w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\times\partial^{\alpha}\eqref{f-R-vector}
with
|\alpha|=N_{m}+1
and integrating the resulting identity over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}+\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu}+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\|^{2}

(4.90)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m},1}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(I_{mix}(t)+I_{pure}(t)\right),w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m},1}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left(\epsilon q_{0}\left\{v\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{m,\epsilon}\right),w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m},1}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{3}}.

For
\mathcal{W}_{1}
, multiplying a time factor
(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}
implies

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\mathcal{W}_{1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}}\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m},1}(\alpha,0)\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(4.91)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}E^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu}.

For
\mathcal{W}_{2}
, setting (
4.15
), (
4.20
),(
4.21
) with
\kappa=1
, which combining with (
4.42
), (
4.43
), (
4.37
), (
4.38
), and (
5.13
) imply that

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{2}

(4.92)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-3}_{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1,\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+2-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right\|_{\nu}^{2}+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\vartheta}\left\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|=1}\sum_{1\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\sum_{1\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t).

For
\mathcal{W}_{3}
, it is easy to see that

\mathcal{W}_{3}=0.

For later simplicity in presentation, we use

\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}=\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2},

(4.93)

thus we get

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}\right\}+(1+t)^{-1-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}

(4.94)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu}+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}(1+t)^{2+2\vartheta}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}]\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-3}_{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1,\atop k\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+2-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}

\displaystyle\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\chi_{|\alpha|=1}\sum_{1\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\sum_{1\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}}\chi_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}E^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}+\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu},

where we have used the assumption (
4.88
).

Secondly, applying
\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}
with
|\alpha|+|\beta|=N_{m}+1,\ |\beta|\geq 1
to (
4.7
),
multiplying the resulting identity by
w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}
, and integrating the final result over
\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|w_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}+\|w_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu}

(4.95)

\displaystyle+\frac{q\vartheta}{(1+t)^{1+\vartheta}}\|w_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta\|w_{l^{*}_{m},1}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}+\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\|_{\nu}^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{-{\bf\{I-P\}}v\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{m,\epsilon}\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\left\{E^{m,\epsilon}+\epsilon b^{f^{m,\epsilon}}\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot v\mu^{1/2}q_{1}\right\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon})\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{5}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left(I_{mix,mic}(t)+I_{pure,mic}(t)\right),w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{6}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\left(\epsilon q_{0}\left\{v\times\left\{B^{P}+\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}B^{j_{1}}\right\}\right\}\cdot\nabla_{v}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{7}}.

By the macro-micro decomposition (
2.10
), one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{W}_{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{4,1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\{v\cdot\nabla_{x}{\bf P}f^{m,\epsilon}\},w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|}_{\mathcal{W}_{4,2}}
