\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+2-\frac{l^{*}_{m}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq|\beta|\leq n,0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{*}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}+1}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,|\beta|}}\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\|\nabla^{n+1}f^{m,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{\nu}.

Step 3:
Based on the above two steps, one has (
4.89
) by a proper linear combination of (
4.99
) and (
4.100
),
which completes the proof of this lemma.
∎

Moreover, we can deduce by applying a similar strategy that

Lemma 4.19
.

Under the assumptions in the above lemma, then

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)\right\}

(4.101)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}\left\{(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop|\alpha|+|\beta|=n}\frac{1}{4C_{\beta}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\frac{d}{dt}\left\|\left\langle\partial_{\beta}\left\{w^{2}_{l^{*}_{m}-|\beta|,1}\partial_{\beta}\left[v_{i}\mu^{1/2}\right]\right\},\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{+}-P_{+}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)-\partial^{\alpha}{\{\bf I_{-}-P_{-}\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\rangle\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}\sum_{0\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,n\},\atop k\leq j\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}+2+2\vartheta}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\left\|\nabla^{3}_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon},E^{j_{1},\epsilon},\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}-3}_{x}}\right\}

\displaystyle\times\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+3-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{m}+2,N_{m}+2-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}+1-N_{m}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},N_{m}+2,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{m}+1,-\frac{l_{m}^{\sharp}+2}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},N_{m},-\gamma}(t)\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}+1}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq\max\{n,2\}}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq\max\{n,2\}}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},N^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}+1}(t).

4.7.
The proof of Theorem
2.2

Now we are ready to close the
a priori
estimates

\displaystyle\Xi_{Total}(t)

(4.102)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(\tau)+\sum_{N^{0}_{m}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(\tau)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(\tau)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(\tau)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(\tau)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{0<i<m}\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{i},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+\tau)^{-{\sigma}_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{i,\epsilon},n,l_{i}^{*},1}(\tau)+\sum_{0<i<m}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},l_{i},-\gamma}(\tau)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{P},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+\tau)^{-{\sigma}_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l^{*}_{P},1}(\tau)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}\lesssim M,

where all parameters satisfy the assumptions listed in Lemma
4.20
and
M
is a sufficiently small positive constant independent of
\epsilon
.

In fact, we can get that

Lemma 4.20
.

Under the a priori estimates (
4.102
) and the assumptions listed in Theorem
2.2
, then it holds that

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbb{Y}_{\textrm{Total},0}^{2}

(4.103)

for all
0\leq t\leq T
. Here
\mathbb{Y}_{\textrm{Total},0}
is defined in (
2.49
).

Proof.

In fact, firstly, we have from Proposition
5.1
and Proposition
5.2
under the
a priori
estimates (
4.102
) that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim 0,

(4.104)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim 0,\ 1\leq j\leq m-1,

from which, we can deduce that:

(i).

If we take

\sigma_{n,0}=0,n\leq N_{m},\ \ \sigma_{N_{m}+1,0}=\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2},

and notice that

\sigma_{n,k}-\sigma_{n,k-1}=\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta),

we can deduce that

\displaystyle\max_{N^{0}_{m}+1\leq n\leq N_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}\{\sigma_{n,k}\}=\sigma_{N_{m},N_{m}},\quad\max_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}\{\sigma_{n,k}\}=\sigma_{N^{0}_{m},N^{0}_{m}};

(ii).

If we take
\widetilde{\ell}_{m}\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}+\frac{(2-4\gamma)\sigma_{N_{m},N_{m}}}{1+\varrho}
and
l_{1}^{*}\geq\widetilde{\ell}_{1}-\frac{\gamma}{2}-\gamma l_{1}
,
then
{\theta}=\frac{2-4\gamma}{2\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma}\leq\frac{1+\varrho}{\sigma_{N_{m},N_{m}}}
and we have from Lemma
4.16
that

\displaystyle\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[E^{P,\epsilon}(t),E^{j_{1},\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{{\theta}}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq N_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,-\gamma l_{m}+\widetilde{l}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon}(t),\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right]\right\|_{H^{N_{m}^{0}-1}_{x}}^{\frac{2}{{\theta}}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq N_{m},\atop k\leq j\leq N_{m}}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\right\}^{\frac{1}{{\theta}}}(1+t)^{-\frac{1+\varrho}{{\theta}}}\sum_{0\leq j\leq N_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq j}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\right\}^{\frac{1}{{\theta}}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m},N_{m}}}\sum_{0\leq j\leq N_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq j}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t).

Thus we have from Lemma
4.11
and (
4.104
) that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

(4.105)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m},l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\left\{\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m},N_{m}}}\sum_{0\leq j\leq N_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq j}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{*},1}^{(j,k)}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l_{m}+\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{\gamma},1}(t).

(iii).

Similarly, we have by taking
l_{m}^{\sharp}\geq\widetilde{\ell}_{m}-\gamma l^{0}_{m}+1+\frac{\gamma}{2}
that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

(4.106)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{0}_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\eta\left\|\nabla^{N_{m}^{0}+1}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}_{\nu}+\left\{\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\right\}^{\frac{1}{\theta}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N^{0}_{m},N^{0}_{m}}}\sum_{0\leq j\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq j}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}^{(j,k)}(t).

(iv).

Since Lemma
4.16
tells us that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\nabla_{v}\nabla^{N_{m}}_{x}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

(4.107)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2m}\epsilon^{-5-2\varrho}(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho}\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)(1+t)^{\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\mathcal{D}^{(N_{m}+1,1)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}^{*},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2m-5-2\varrho}(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho+\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\mathcal{D}^{(N_{m}+1,1)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}^{*},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho+\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}+\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta)}\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\mathcal{D}^{(N_{m}+1,1)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}^{*},1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\mathcal{D}^{(N_{m}+1,1)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}^{*},1},

where we used the fact that

\epsilon^{2m-5-2\varrho}+(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho+\frac{1+\epsilon_{0}}{2}+\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta)}\lesssim 1

provided that

m\geq\frac{5}{2}+\varrho,\ 0<\vartheta\leq\frac{\gamma-2}{2+2\gamma}\left(2+\varrho-\frac{\epsilon_{0}}{2}\right)-1,

then if we take
l_{m}^{*}\geq N_{m}+2-\frac{\gamma}{2}
, we can get from Lemma
4.12
that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

(4.108)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1}\mathcal{D}^{(N_{m}+1,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,l_{m}^{*},1}.

(v).

By Lemma
4.16
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}+2+2\vartheta}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\|\nabla_{x}\left[\epsilon^{m}E^{m,\epsilon},\epsilon^{m+1}B^{m,\epsilon}\right](t)\right\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,n\},\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2m}\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}+2+2\vartheta}\epsilon^{-5-2\varrho}(1+t)^{-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho}

\displaystyle\times\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\sum_{0\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,n\},\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2m-5-2\varrho}\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}(1+t)^{\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta)+2+2\vartheta-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho}

\displaystyle\times\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\sum_{0\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,n\},\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2m-5-2\varrho}\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|+1}}

\displaystyle\times\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\sum_{0\leq k\leq\min\{|\beta|+1,n\},\atop k\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,l^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l^{*}_{m},1}(t),

where we have used the fact that

\epsilon^{2m-5-2\varrho}+(1+t)^{\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta)+2+2\vartheta-\frac{5}{2}-\varrho}\lesssim 1

provided that

m\geq\frac{5}{2}+\varrho,\ 0<\vartheta\leq\frac{\gamma-2}{4\gamma-2}\left(\frac{5}{2}+\varrho\right)-1.

Thus one deduces that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

(4.109)

\displaystyle+\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\eta(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\left\|\epsilon\nabla^{N_{m}+1}E^{m,\epsilon}(t)\right\|^{2}+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)\mathcal{E}^{1}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m},l^{*}_{m}+1-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l^{*}_{m},1}(t),

where we have used the fact that

\displaystyle\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,|\beta|}}\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n}\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k_{1}}}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k_{2}}}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{N_{m}^{0}+1\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq|\beta|\leq n,k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{j_{1},k_{1}}}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{j_{2},k_{2}}}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},l_{m}^{*},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{m}+1,\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l^{*}_{m},1}(t).

(vi)

In spirit of (
4.109
), one also deduce

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

(4.110)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N^{0}_{m},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}\left\{(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,|\beta|)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{*},1}(t)+(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{D}^{(n,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m}^{\sharp},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N^{0}_{m}+1}(t).

(vii).

Multiplying
(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}
to (
4.108
), one gets

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\right\}

(4.111)

\displaystyle+(1+t)^{-1-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)+(1+t)^{-\epsilon_{0}}\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1}(t)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{\overline{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{\overline{D}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)(1+t)^{-\sigma_{N_{m}+1,1}}\sum_{0\leq k\leq 1}\mathcal{D}^{(N_{m}+1,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},N_{m}+1,N_{m}+2-\frac{\gamma}{2},1}.

With the above preparations in hand, by taking a proper linear combination of (
4.105
), (
4.106
), (
4.108
), (
4.109
), (
4.110
), and (
4.111
) and by
applying the smallness of
\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)
and
\eta
, we can deduce by taking the time integration with respect to
t
from
0
to
t
that the resulting differential inequality that
(
4.103
) holds. Thus the proof of Lemma
4.20
is complete.
∎

Now we turn to prove Theorem
2.2
. To this end, recall the definition of the
\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)-
norm. Lemma
4.20
tells that for the local solution
[f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{m,\epsilon}(t,x),B^{m,\epsilon}(t,x)]
to the Cauchy problem (
2.31
)-(
2.34
) defined on the time interval
[0,T]
for some
0<T\leq+\infty
, if

\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\leq M

holds for all
t\in[0,T]
and some
\epsilon-
independent positive constant
M
, then there exists a sufficiently small
\epsilon-
independent positive constant
\delta_{0}>0
such that if

M\leq\delta^{2}_{0},

there exists a
\epsilon-
independent positive constant
\overline{C}>0
such that

\Xi_{\textrm{Total}}(t)\leq\overline{C}^{2}\mathbb{Y}_{\textrm{Total},0}^{2}

holds for all
0\leq t\leq T
.

Thus if the initial perturbation
\mathbb{Y}_{\textrm{Total},0}
is assumed to be sufficiently small such that

\mathbb{Y}_{\textrm{Total},0}\leq\frac{\delta_{0}}{\overline{C}},

then the global existence follows by combining the local solvability result with the continuation argument in the usual way.
To obtain (
2.50
),
recall that the expansion form (
2.1.2
), we can deduce that from (
4.103
)

\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{m}}\left\|\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\frac{F^{\epsilon}-F^{P}}{\mu^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right\}\right\|^{2}+\|E^{\epsilon}-E^{P,\epsilon}\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}+1}_{x}}+\|B^{\epsilon}-B^{P}\|^{2}_{H^{N_{m}+1}_{x}}\lesssim\epsilon^{2m}\mathbb{Y}_{\textrm{Total},0}^{2},

(4.112)

that is what we want. Thus the proof of Theorem
2.2
is complete.∎

5.
Appendix

5.1.
Appendix A

In this section, we collect several basic estimates on the linearized Boltzmann collision operator
L
and the nonlinear term
\Gamma
for hard sphere interaction. Since these estimates are well-established in
[
15
,
17
,
32
]
for hard sphere model and in
[
11
,
33
]
for cutoff potentials, we only state the result and omit the proofs for brevity.

L
is locally coercive in the sense that

Lemma 5.1
.

(cf.
[
5
, Lemma 3.1]
)
Let
-3<\gamma\leq 1
, one has

\langle Lf,f\rangle\geq|{\bf\{I-P\}}f|^{2}_{\nu}.

(5.1)

Moreover, let
|\beta|>0
, for
\eta>0
small enough and any
\ell\in\mathbb{R},\kappa\geq 0,0<q\ll 1,\vartheta\in\mathbb{R}
, there exists
C_{\eta}>0
such that

\left\langle w_{\ell,\kappa}^{2}\partial_{\beta}{L}f,\partial_{\beta}f\right\rangle\geq\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial_{\beta}f\right|^{2}_{\nu}-\eta\sum_{|\beta^{\prime}|<|\beta|}\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial_{\beta^{\prime}}\{{\bf I-P}\}f\right|_{\nu}^{2}-C_{\eta}\left|\chi_{\{|v|\leq 2C_{\eta}\}}f\right|^{2}

(5.2)

holds.

The second lemma is concerned with the corresponding weighted estimates on the nonlinear term
\Gamma
.
For this purpose, similar to that of
[
33
]
, we can get that

\displaystyle\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\Gamma_{\pm}(g_{1},g_{2})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum C_{\beta}^{\beta_{0}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}{\Gamma}^{0}_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1},\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right)

(5.3)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum C_{\beta}^{\beta_{0}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}C_{\alpha}^{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{S}^{2}}|v-u|^{\gamma}{\bf b}(\cos\theta)\partial_{\beta_{0}}[\mu(u)^{\frac{1}{2}}]\left\{\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\pm}(u^{\prime})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v^{\prime})\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\mp}(u^{\prime})-\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v)\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\pm}(u)-\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1\pm}(v)\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2\mp}(u)\right\}d\omega du,

where
g_{i}(t,x,v)=[g_{i+}(t,x,v),g_{i-}(t,x,v)]

(i=1,2)
and the summations are taken for all
\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta,\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha
.

Lemma 5.2
.

(cf.
[
5
, Lemma 3.2]
)
Assume
\kappa\geq 0,\ell\geq 0
.
Let
-3<\gamma\leq 1
,
N\geq 4
,
g_{i}=g_{i}(t,x,v)=[g_{i+}(t,x,v),g_{i-}(t,x,v)]\ (i=1,2,3)
,
\beta_{0}+\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}=\beta
and
\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}=\alpha
, we have
the following results:

(i).

When
|\alpha_{1}|+|\beta_{1}|\leq N
, we have

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\langle w_{\ell,\kappa}^{2}{\Gamma}^{0}_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1},\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right\rangle\lesssim\sum\limits_{m\leq 2}\left\{\left|\nabla^{m}_{v}\left\{\mu^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right\}\right|+\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right|\right\}\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\end{array}

(5.4)

or

\begin{array}[]{rl}\left\langle w_{\ell,\kappa}^{2}{\Gamma}^{0}_{\pm}\left(\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1},\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right),\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right\rangle\lesssim\sum\limits_{m\leq 2}\left\{\left|\nabla^{m}_{v}\left\{\mu^{\delta}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right\}\right|+\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha_{2}}_{\beta_{2}}g_{2}\right|\right\}\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha_{1}}_{\beta_{1}}g_{1}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|w_{\ell,\kappa}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}g_{3}\right|_{L^{2}_{\nu}}.\end{array}

(5.5)

(ii).

Set
\varsigma(v)=\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma}\equiv\nu(v)^{-1},~l\geq 0
, it holds that

\begin{split}\left|\varsigma^{l}{\Gamma}(g_{1},g_{2})\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}&\lesssim\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}\left|\varsigma^{l-|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}g_{1}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|\varsigma^{l}g_{2}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}},\\
\left|\varsigma^{l}{\Gamma}(g_{1},g_{2})\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{v}}&\lesssim\sum_{|\beta|\leq 2}\left|\varsigma^{l}g_{1}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}\left|\varsigma^{l-|\beta|}\partial_{\beta}g_{2}\right|^{2}_{L^{2}_{\nu}}.\end{split}

(5.6)

Based on Lemma
5.2
, we can get some weighted estimates on the interactions between
f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),f^{1,\epsilon}(t,x,v),

\cdots,f^{m,\epsilon}(t,x,v)
with respect to the weight
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
, whose proof will be omitted for brevity.

Lemma 5.3
.

Take
n\geq 8
and
w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}=\langle v\rangle^{-\gamma(l_{m}-|\beta|)}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}
, one has the following estimates:

i)

For
|\alpha|\leq n
with
j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,0<j_{1},j_{2}<m
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\}\right.\right.

(5.7)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n}(t)+\eta\left\|\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2},

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\epsilon^{m}\Gamma(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon})\right\},\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(5.8)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t);

ii)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\}\right.\right.

(5.9)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{\epsilon^{m}\Gamma(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon})\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m},-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(5.10)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t);

iii)

For
|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\}\right.\right.

(5.11)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)

\displaystyle+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n,l_{i},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}(t)\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}

and

\displaystyle\left|\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{\epsilon^{m}\Gamma(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon})\right\},w^{2}_{l_{m}-|\beta|,-\gamma}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right|

(5.12)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n,l_{m},-\gamma}(t).

For the corresponding estimates with respect to the weight
w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}(t,v)
, we have

Lemma 5.4
.

Take
n\geq 8
and
w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}(t,v)=\langle v\rangle^{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|}e^{\frac{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}{(1+t)^{\vartheta}}}
, one has the following estimates:

i)

For
1\leq|\alpha|\leq n
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\}\right.\right.

(5.13)

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)+\epsilon^{m}\Gamma(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon})\right\},w^{2}_{\ell_{m}^{*},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|=1}\sum_{1\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)\sum_{1\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{D}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|\geq 2}\sum_{0\leq j_{1}\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{E}^{(j_{1},0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{1},\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)\times\sum_{0\leq j_{2}\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{D}^{(j_{2},0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j_{2},\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)

\displaystyle+\left\{\sum_{0\leq j\leq|\alpha|}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*},1}\partial^{\alpha}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2};

iii)

For
1\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq n
with
|\beta|\geq 1
or
|\alpha|=|\beta|=0
, one has

\displaystyle\left(\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{P,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{P,\epsilon}\right)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\epsilon^{j_{1}}\left\{{\Gamma}\left(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right)+{\Gamma}\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon}\right)\right\}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\epsilon^{j_{1}+j_{2}-m}\Gamma\left(f^{j_{1},\epsilon},f^{j_{2},\epsilon}\right)+\epsilon^{m}\Gamma(f^{m,\epsilon},f^{m,\epsilon})\right\},w^{2}_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\{\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n-1,n-1,-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle\times\left\{\sum_{0\leq k\leq|\beta|,\atop 0\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\left\{\sum_{0\leq j\leq n}\mathcal{E}^{(j,0)}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)+\mathcal{E}_{f^{m,\epsilon},n}(t)\right\}\left\{\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{1\leq j_{1}\leq m-1}\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq 1}\sum_{0\leq k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{k_{1}\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{E}^{(j,k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)\sum_{k_{2}\leq j\leq 2}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k_{2})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\chi_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\geq 2}\sum_{0\leq k_{1}+k_{2}\leq|\beta|}\left\{\sum_{k_{1}\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{E}^{(j,k_{1})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell_{m}^{*},1}(t)\sum_{k_{2}\leq j\leq|\alpha|+|\beta|}\mathcal{D}^{(j,k_{2})}_{f^{m,\epsilon},j,\ell^{*}_{m},1}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1}+j_{2}\geq m,\atop 0<j_{1},j_{2}<m}\mathcal{E}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)\mathcal{D}_{f^{j_{2},\epsilon},n,-\frac{\ell_{m}^{*}}{\gamma},-\gamma}(t)+\eta\left\|w_{\ell_{m}^{*}-|\beta|,1}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}{\bf\{I-P\}}f^{m,\epsilon}\langle v\rangle^{\frac{\gamma}{2}}\right\|^{2}.

5.2.
Appendix B

To deduce the desired energy estimates, we have to obtain the corresponding energy estimates with respect to
f^{P,\epsilon}
and
f^{i,\epsilon}
for
1\leq i\leq m
.
The first result is concerned with the estimates on
f^{P,\epsilon}
.

To this end, define

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total},P}(t)\equiv\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{P},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+\tau)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l^{*}_{P},1}(\tau)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}\lesssim M_{P},

(5.14)

where
M_{P}
is a sufficiently small positive constant independent of
\epsilon
.

Under the
a priori
estimates (
5.14
), we have

Proposition 5.1
.

Take

•

-3<\gamma<-1
,
\varrho\in[\frac{1}{2},\frac{3}{2})
,
N_{P}\geq 5
;

•

\vartheta
satisfies

0<\vartheta\leq\min\left\{\frac{\gamma-2}{4\gamma-2}\left(\frac{5}{2}+\varrho\right)-1,\frac{\varrho}{2}-\frac{1}{4}\right\};

•

{\sigma}_{n,0}=0
for
n\leq N_{P}
and

{\sigma}_{n,k}-{\sigma}_{n,k-1}=\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta),1\leq k\leq n,1\leq n\leq N_{P};

•

there exists
\widehat{l}_{P}>\frac{N_{P}+\varrho}{2}
,
\ell_{P}\geq N_{P},\ l_{P}\geq\ell_{P}+\widehat{l}_{P}
,
\widetilde{\ell}_{P}\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{2\gamma{\sigma}_{N_{P},N_{P}}}{1+\varrho}
and
l_{P}^{*}\geq\widetilde{\ell}_{P}-\gamma l_{P}
;

•

F^{P,0}(x,v)=\mu+\sqrt{\mu}f_{P,0}(x,v)\geq 0
.

If we assume further that

\begin{split}\mathbb{Y}_{P,0}=&\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{P}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{l_{P}^{*}-|\beta|}e^{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{0}^{P,\epsilon}\right\|+\left\|\left[f_{0}^{P,\epsilon},E_{0}^{P,\epsilon}\right]\right\|_{H^{-\varrho}_{x}}\end{split}

(5.15)

is bounded from above by some sufficiently small positive constant independent of
\epsilon
, the Cauchy problem (
2.36
), (
2.37
) admits a unique global solution
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
satisfying
F^{P}(t,x,v)=\mu+\sqrt{\mu}f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v)\geq 0
. Furthermore, one also has

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim 0,

(5.16)

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)\lesssim 0,\ k=0,1,\cdots,N_{P}-1,

(5.17)

\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{P},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{P},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim 0,\ k=0,1,\cdots,N_{P}-2,

(5.18)

and

(1+t)^{\varrho+k}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)\lesssim\mathbb{Y}_{P,0}^{2},\ k=0,1,\cdots,N_{P}-1,

(5.19)

(1+t)^{\varrho+k}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{P},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim\mathbb{Y}_{P,0}^{2},\ k=0,1,\cdots,N_{P}-2,

(5.20)

(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l^{*}_{P},1}(t)\lesssim\mathbb{Y}_{P,0}^{2},\ k=0,1,\cdots,n.

(5.21)

where
0\leq n\leq N_{P}
.

Proof.

In fact,
\left[F^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
satisfy the Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann system with a given magnetic filed, this proposition can be obtained by a similar argument as
[
5
,
23
]
. We omit its proof for simplicity.
∎

Based on the above proposition, for
i=1,2,\cdots,m-1
, we can obtain the corresponding results on the global solvability of the Cauchy problem (
2.38
)-(
2.41
) together with some estimates on its global solution
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
by the method of mathematical induction.

In fact, if we define

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total},i}(t)

(5.22)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\left\{\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{i},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+\tau)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{i,\epsilon},n,l_{i}^{*},1}(\tau)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},l_{i},-\gamma}(\tau)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{0<j_{1}<i}\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{j_{1}},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+\tau)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l^{*}_{j_{1}},1}(\tau)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{0<j_{1}<i}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l_{j_{1}},-\gamma}(\tau)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\quad\quad+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{P},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+\tau)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l^{*}_{P},1}(\tau)+\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l_{P},-\gamma}(\tau)\right\}\lesssim M_{i},

where
M_{i}
is a sufficiently small positive constant independent of
\epsilon
, we can get by repeating the arguments used in deducing Lemma
4.16
, Lemma
4.17
, Lemma
4.20
, and by the method of mathematical induction that

Proposition 5.2
.

Assume

•

\gamma\in(-3,-1)
,
\varrho\in\left[1,\frac{3}{2}\right)
;

•

\vartheta
satisfies

0<\vartheta\leq\min\left\{\frac{\gamma-2}{4\gamma-2}\left(\frac{5}{2}+\varrho\right)-1,\frac{\varrho}{2}-\frac{1}{4}\right\};

•

N_{P},N_{i}
and
{\sigma}_{n,k}
can be taken as

–

N_{i}\geq 5
,
N_{j}\geq N_{j+1}+1
for
1\leq j\leq i-1
,
N_{P}\geq N_{1}+1
;

–

{\sigma}_{n,0}=0
for
n\leq N_{i}
and

{\sigma}_{n,k}-{\sigma}_{n,k-1}=\frac{2(1+\gamma)}{\gamma-2}(1+\vartheta),1\leq k\leq n,1\leq n\leq N_{i};

•

–

there exists
\widehat{l}_{i}>\frac{N_{P}+\varrho}{2}
,
\widehat{l}_{j-1}>\widehat{l}_{j}
for
1<j\leq i
,
\widehat{l}_{P}>\widehat{l}_{1}

where
\widehat{l}_{P}
satisfying the assumptions in Proposition
5.1
;

–

\widetilde{\ell}_{i}\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{2\gamma{\sigma}_{N_{i},N_{i}}}{1+\varrho}
,
\ell_{i}\geq N_{i}
,
l_{i}\geq\ell_{i}+\widehat{l}_{i}
and
l_{i}^{*}\geq\widetilde{\ell}_{i}-\gamma l_{i}
;

–

\widetilde{\ell}_{j}\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{2\gamma{\sigma}_{N_{j},N_{j}}}{1+\varrho}
,
\ell_{j}\geq-\frac{l_{j+1}^{*}}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{\gamma}
,
l_{j}\geq\ell_{j}+\widehat{l}_{j}
and
l_{j}^{*}\geq\widetilde{\ell}_{j}-\gamma l_{j}
for
1\leq j\leq i-1
;

–

\widetilde{\ell}_{P}\geq\frac{\gamma}{2}-\frac{2\gamma{\sigma}_{N_{P},N_{P}}}{1+\varrho}
,

\ell_{P}\geq-\frac{l_{1}^{*}}{\gamma}+\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2}{\gamma}
,
l_{P}\geq\ell_{P}+\widehat{l}_{P}
and
l_{P}^{*}\geq\widetilde{\ell}_{P}-\gamma l_{P}
.

If we assume further that

\displaystyle\mathbb{Y}_{i,0}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{i}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{l_{i}^{*}-|\beta|}e^{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{0}^{i,\epsilon}\right\|+\left\|E_{0}^{i,\epsilon}\right\|_{H^{N_{i}}_{x}}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left[f_{0}^{i,\epsilon},E_{0}^{i,\epsilon}\right]\right\|

(5.23)

\displaystyle+\sum_{0<j_{1}<i}\left\{\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{j_{1}}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{l_{j_{1}}^{*}-|\beta|}e^{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{0}^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right\|+\left\|E_{0}{j_{1},\epsilon}\right\|_{H^{N_{j_{1}}}_{x}}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left[f_{0}^{j_{1},\epsilon},E_{0}^{j_{1},\epsilon}\right]\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\alpha|+|\beta|\leq N_{P}}\left\|\langle v\rangle^{l_{P}^{*}-|\beta|}e^{q\langle v\rangle^{2}}\partial^{\alpha}_{\beta}f_{0}^{P,\epsilon}\right\|+\left\|E_{0}^{P,\epsilon}\right\|_{H^{N_{P}}_{x}}+\left\|\Lambda^{-\varrho}\left[f_{0}^{P,\epsilon},E_{0}^{P,\epsilon}\right]\right\|

is bounded from above by some sufficiently small positive constant independent of
\epsilon
, then the Cauchy problem (
2.38
)-(
2.41
) admits a unique global solution
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
which satisfies

\displaystyle\Xi_{\textrm{Total},i}(t)\lesssim\mathbb{Y}_{i,0}^{2}.

(5.24)

Moreover, one also has

\frac{d}{dt}\overline{\mathcal{E}}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},l_{i},-\gamma}(t)+\overline{\mathcal{D}}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},l_{i},-\gamma}(t)\lesssim 0,\quad\quad 0<i<m

(5.25)

and for
k=0,1,2,\cdots,N_{i}-1
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq i-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i}}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq i-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\leq 0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T.

(5.26)

As a consequence of (
5.2
), we can get that

\displaystyle(1+t)^{k+\varrho}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i}}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P}}(t)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq i-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j}}(t)\right\}\lesssim\mathbb{Y}^{2}_{i,0},

(5.27)

and for
k=0,1,2,\cdots,N_{i}-2
, then one has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq i-1}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)\right\}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{1\leq j\leq i-1}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)\leq 0,\quad 0\leq t\leq T,

(5.28)

as a consequence of (
5.2
), we can get that

\begin{split}(1+t)^{k+\varrho}\left\{\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{i,\epsilon},N_{i},{\ell_{i}},-\gamma}(t)+\sum_{0<j<i}\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{j,\epsilon},N_{j},{\ell_{i}},-\gamma}(t)+\mathcal{E}^{k}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},\ell_{i},-\gamma}(t)\right\}\lesssim&\mathbb{Y}^{2}_{i,0}.\end{split}

(5.29)

Furthermore, for
i=1,2,\cdots,m-1
, one can get that

\displaystyle\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{i},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}\mathcal{E}^{(n,k)}_{f^{i,\epsilon},n,l_{i}^{*},1}(t)+\chi_{i\geq 2}\sum_{0<j_{1}<i}\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{j_{1}},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{j_{1},\epsilon},N_{j_{1}},l^{*}_{j_{1}},1}(t)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{0\leq n\leq N_{P},\atop 0\leq k\leq n}(1+t)^{-\sigma_{n,k}}{\mathcal{E}}^{(n,k)}_{f^{P,\epsilon},N_{P},l^{*}_{P},1}(t)\lesssim\mathbb{Y}^{2}_{i,0}.

(5.30)

Remark 5.1
.

For the Cauchy problem (
2.36
), (
2.37
) and the Cauchy problem (
2.38
)-(
2.41
) for the whole range of cutoff intermolecular interactions, since both equations under our considerations are Vlasov-Poisson-Boltzmann type equations with given constant magnetic field, the arguments used to deduce Lemma
4.16
, Lemma
4.17
, Lemma
4.20
can be adopted to yield the desired results listed in Propositions
5.1
and
5.2
by employing two sets of weighted energy estimates with the weights
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
and
w_{\ell-|\beta|,1}(t,v)
respectively. Although the estimates obtained in Propositions
5.1
and
5.2
are sufficient to prove Theorem
2.2
, these results can indeed be improved.

In fact, for the moderately soft potentials (i.e. for the case of
-2\leq\gamma<0
), by employing the argument developed in
[
11
]
, we can use a single set of weighted energy method with weights
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
to deduce similar global solvability results for both the Cauchy problem (
2.36
), (
2.37
) and the Cauchy problem (
2.38
)-(
2.41
) and to deduce nice temporal decay estimates on
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
and
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
for
i=1,2,\cdots,m-1
which are sufficient to prove Theorem
2.2
, provided that the initial data are assumed
to satisfy the neutral conditions.

For the very soft potentials (i.e. for the case of
-3<\gamma<-2
), by employing the argument developed in
[
39
]
, we can still use a single set of weighted energy method with weights
w_{\ell-|\beta|,-\gamma}(t,v)
to get similar global solvability results for both the Cauchy problem (
2.36
), (
2.37
) and the Cauchy problem (
2.38
)-(
2.41
) and to deduce the desired temporal decay estimates. But, as in
[
39
]
, we had to deduce the almost optimal decay estimates on
\left[f^{P,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{P,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
and
\left[f^{i,\epsilon}(t,x,v),E^{i,\epsilon}(t,x)\right]
for
i=1,2,\cdots,m-1
themselves, certain orders of derivatives with respect the space and velocity variables, and even the macroscopic component and microscopic component of them. Since such an improvement will lead to quite complex analysis, we thus state the somewhat weaker results in Propositions
5.1
and
5.2
for simplicity of presentation.
